# Compact launch against the full grid: 128x128

Kernel BLOCK 128x128x64, 4 waves, 256 programs. 3 launches per mode plus 2 recorded. grid: full / compact; programs: the stream-K programs the host passes (the request capped at one per pair, S x pairs), all working; C: bit-identical (or both correct, for atomic_iz and bf16 partials); XCC rotations r (workgroup p on XCC (p + r) % 8); CUs: distinct CUs / working programs per XCD, compact launch; at most per CU.
